# History Archive

Historical content moved out of `DeribitIndicatorProject.md` on 2026-05-17 to keep the operational handover under the Read-tool token cap. **Operational reference lives in `DeribitIndicatorProject.md`.** This file is for retrospective lookups only — pre-v30 settings change rationale, full version history, completed bundles, and resolved parked observations.

For settings change details post-v22, read the `change_log` array inside `settings.json` directly. It's the source of truth.

---

## A. Pre-v22 settings.json Migration Notes

### v21 (RSI divergence + ROC split, 2026-04-30)
`CalcRSIDivergence` semantically rewritten: BEARISH fires when current price is AT OR ABOVE prior pivot (canonical higher-high pattern), prior pivot's RSI must have been ≥ `DivergenceOverboughtThreshold` (65) to qualify as exhaustion, most-recent confirmed pivot is used rather than highest in lookback. Mirror fix for BULLISH. `DivergenceRsiDelta` raised 2.0 → 5.0. Expected NONE rate rises from ~20% to ~80–90%. `ROC.slope_sensitivity` split into `slope_delta_threshold` (0.05, for RISING/FALLING/FLAT bar-to-bar classification) and `magnitude_threshold` (0.1, for partial ROC scoring and Pass 2c ROC-active check). Old key removed.

### v20 (OI threshold recalibration, 2026-04-30)
Post-v19 499-row dataset showed `OISignal` still 100% NEUTRAL: the effective threshold (0.003 × 100 = 0.3%) was above the observed 15m OI peak of ~0.23%. `indicators.OI.change_threshold_pct` lowered 0.003 → 0.002 (effective 0.2%). Quiet-session noise ~0.01–0.015% → 10:1 signal/noise separation.

### v19 (calibration tuning pass, 2026-04-30)
Six classifiers stuck on a single value across 618 rows due to threshold mismatches vs observed BTC-PERPETUAL scale. Recalibrated: `scoring.funding_high_positive/negative` ±3 bp → ±0.3 bp; `scoring.funding_low_positive/negative` ±0.5 bp → ±0.05 bp; `indicators.funding.momentum_threshold` 10 bp → 0.5 bp; `indicators.OI.change_threshold_pct` 1.0% → 0.3%; `indicators.ROC.slope_sensitivity` 0.1 → 0.05. `GetRecentTradesAsync(100)` → `GetRecentTradesAsync(500)` to widen liquidation detection window from ~60s to ~5min.

### v18 (API resilience pass, 2026-04-30)
`DeribitClient` wraps all five `GetXxxAsync` methods with `ExecuteWithRetry`: retry-once with 1s backoff on transient failures (5xx, timeout, network drop); return `Nothing` on hard failure. `GetFundingRateAsync` return type `Double` → `Double?`; `GetBookSummaryAsync` value tuple → nullable value tuple. `RunAnalysisAsync` validates all required fetches after `Task.WhenAll`; if any are `Nothing`, renders `ANALYSIS SKIPPED: <reason>`, increments `_skipCount`, returns without scoring or writing a CSV row. 15m cache preserved on fetch failure — stale data kept for MTF gate.

### v17 (settings-exposure pass, 2026-04-29)
All new keys default to exactly the previously-hardcoded values — no behavioural change. Keys added: `rsi.pass2c_midline`, `bbw.series_window_multiplier`, `bbw.squeeze_percentile`, `donchian.quartile_pct`, `cvd.late_segment_weight`, `cvd.early_segment_weight`, `ttm.sma_period`, `ttm.lin_reg_period`, `scoring.regime_max_score`, `scoring.tier_floor`, `scoring.context_tag_thresholds`. Closes the auto-tweaking audit prerequisite.

### v16 (swing pivot spec, 2026-04-29)
Added `indicators.swing` block: `pivot_wing_5m`, `lookback_bars_5m`, `pivot_wing_15m`, `lookback_bars_15m`.

### v15 cleanup (2026-04-27)
Removed silently-ignored keys: `scoring.long_threshold` / `short_threshold` / `strong_long_threshold` / `strong_short_threshold` / `medium_long_threshold` / `medium_short_threshold` (superseded by `verdict_*_pct` since v0.30); `scoring.weights` block (`ScoringWeights` class deleted long ago); `scoring.transitional_penalty_enabled` (TRANSITIONAL ADX penalty applies unconditionally); `regime_gates.suppress_long_in_trending_down` / `suppress_short_in_trending_up` (Step 4 regime veto applies unconditionally); `indicators.EMA200` block (`CalcEMA(candles5m, 200)` is hardcoded); `indicators.OBV.lookback`, `indicators.CVD.trade_lookback`, `indicators.VWAP.dev_threshold_pct`, `indicators.VWAP.session1_start_hour` / `session1_start_minute`, `indicators.BBW.releasing_roc_threshold`, `indicators.Liquidations.long_liq_threshold` / `short_liq_threshold`, `indicators.ATR.ref_period`; `mtf_gate.ema_period_fast` / `ema_period_slow` (`CalcMTFGate` hardcodes EMA 9/21/50). Display-only colour bugs fixed in `MainForm_Render_Sections`. Zero scoring impact.

CalibrationReport reached threshold-with-caveats on 2026-05-05 (2460 rows, 4 regimes covered; 0 liquidation events accepted as rare-event blocker). User authorised proceeding with the full backlog under explicit priority order:

**Bundle 1 (foundation) — shipped 2026-05-05.**
- `csv-expansion-v0.4-proposal.md` — adds 18 columns at positions 69–86 (SpreadBps, OFIMomentum, FundingDelta, VPFR-v2 fields, swing fields, TargetCapReason, BestPivotByVolume reservations). Bumps schema, rotates log on header mismatch.
- `analysis-script-proposal.md` — VB.NET host-agnostic offline analyser at `analysis/`. Forward-return joiner, failure-rate matrix, funding-momentum diagnostic, OFI outlier audit, OI×CVD asymmetry audit. Reachable from MainForm via `lnkAnalysisReport`.

**Bundle 3 (structural refinements) — shipped 2026-05-06.**

- `d2-volume-weighted-pivots-proposal.md` — display-only volume-weighted pivot ranking. v2 cap arbitration parked as observation P1.
- `b1-per-indicator-regime-weights-proposal.md` — STUB, blocked on Bundle 1 output for empirical hit rates.

**Bundle 2 (auto-tweaker) — shipped 2026-05-06.**
- `failure-definition-proposal.md` — ATR-based forward-return failure, 3 windows × 3 thresholds, Wilson-CI cell-stability picker. **Superseded** by `failure-definition-v2-proposal.md` (2026-05-07): barrier-hit with adverse stop, OHLC walk via Deribit.
- `auto-tweaker-pipeline-proposal.md` — VB.NET console app at `tools/AutoTweaker/`, Linux-portable, dry-run mode + manual-apply path. Later extended by `settings-snapshot-history-proposal.md` (2026-05-12) and `auto-tweaker-fixed-window-proposal.md` (2026-05-17).

Bundles 4 (small refinements per B4 item), 5 (multi-session VPFR / anchored VWAP, C1/C2), and 6 (Smart OBV / MFI replacement) remain deferred. Section A (post-WebSocket) and the WebSocket migration itself remain post-Bundle-2 in priority.

## D. Resolved Parked Observations

### P3. OI×CVD asymmetry — RESOLVED 2026-05-08
**Diagnosis:** asymmetry was upstream of Pass 2b. `MainForm_Analysis.vb`'s `priceUp` computation compared `r.CurrentPrice > bookSummary.Value.MarkPrice * 0.9999` — but `MarkPrice` is the current snapshot of mid + smoothing, tracking the last-traded within ~1bp at any moment. The `* 0.9999` factor introduced a 1bp bias on the threshold, so `priceUp` was True almost always. The classification branches `NEW SHORTS` (OI rose + price fell) and `CAPITULATION` (OI fell + price fell) almost never fired, starving Pass 2b's CONFIRMED_SHORT path.
**Fix:** commit `e2ecb95` 2026-05-08. `priceUp` now compares `r.CurrentPrice` against `candles1m(count - 16).Close` — the actual close 15 minutes ago, matching the window of `OIChange15m`. Pass 2b downstream logic was already symmetric.

### P7. Live per-analysis success/fail display — RESOLVED 2026-05-13
Trader confirmed need 2026-05-12 — settings-snapshot work showed that round-level stats (every ~10–15 min) are insufficient for real-time trading-style adaptation.
**Resolution:** implemented per `docs/live-performance-display-proposal.md`. Six-window strip (Cur.Wk / 3d / Cur.Day / Asia / London / NY) in `MainForm`, updated on every `RunAnalysisAsync`. Session most-recent-block semantics. Reuses `FailureRateMatrix.WalkBars` verbatim. Two gitignored sidecar caches. Settings block `performance_display` added to v26.

| **v62 · fee-aware min-move floor** (D1–D6) | 2026-07-27 | **The minimum-tradeable-move floor is now COMPOSED from an execution-cost model instead of a flat literal — defaults byte-identical to v61, so ZERO behaviour change at ship and NOT a dataset boundary** (settings v61 → v62; the v56 arbitration-modes precedent). Spec: `fee-aware-min-move-proposal.md` (APPROVED 2026-07-27, D1–D6 ticked all-as-recommended); spec-back: `fee-aware-min-move-spec-back.md`; order-app relay: `fee-aware-order-app-relay-2026-07-27.md`. **Trigger:** the Deribit fee change effective 2026-08-01 (maker 1.5 bps / taker 3.5 bps, trader-supplied) invalidates the derivation basis of the v35 floor, which was sized "to clear slippage" under zero-maker-fee execution — a re-derivation, not a re-opened settled decision. **Mechanism:** `scoring.min_tradeable_move_pct` RETIRED (JSON key + POCO property removed; applier-unresolvable so C-6 rejects it naturally — deliberately NOT added to `RejectedPathFragments`, the v47-F1 lesson, v53/v61 precedent). New block `scoring.trade_costs` `{maker_fee_bps 1.5, taker_fee_bps 3.5, round_trip_style "maker_maker", min_net_move_pct 0.0005}`, resolved by ONE shared resolver `TradeCostSettings.EffectiveMinMovePct = RoundTripFeePct(style) + MinNetMovePct`, where `RoundTripFeePct` sums **both legs** (maker_maker = 2 × 1.5 bps; maker_taker = maker + taker; taker_taker = 2 × 3.5 bps; unrecognised style falls back to maker_maker). Fees are proportional to notional, so cost and move share the unit "% of price" and the engine never needs trade size; ATR enters nowhere. **Byte-identity at defaults:** 0.0003 + 0.0005 = **0.0008** = the v61 floor exactly — verdicts, eval floors and BELOW_MIN_MOVE rates unchanged at ship. The Aug-1 fee reality is expressed thereafter by turning the min-net knob, which the v35 hot-reload + eval floor-change re-walk machinery already absorbs attributably. **D1:** `maker_maker` is correct rather than optimistic — the floor gates the TARGET side (the profit path: maker entry + maker TP in the trader's flow); taker occurs on emergency SL repositioning and rare manual exits, the LOSS path this floor does not price. **Call sites** all routed through the one resolver: Step 5c, `LivePerformanceTracker` ×4 (incl. `_floorPctInEffect`, now the composed value — same number at defaults, so no re-walk at ship), `AnalysisRunner` ×4, `BandLadder`, CeilingAudit `CsvFeatureBuilder`, `AutoTweakerCore`, `WhatIfReplay`, `WhatIfReport` ×2. **UI:** new editable row **`MIN NET MOVE % (after fees)`** in the SETTINGS & TOOLS card with a derived read-out of the composed floor (v35's change_log claimed UI-editability but no control existed — grep-verified 2026-07-27); commits on Enter/blur via `SettingsLoader.Save(bumpVersion:=False)` (operational save, v36 §10a) → hot-reload next run; re-read after each run so a file-side fee edit surfaces. A live status element ⇒ **display-parity exempt** (the EXIT GUARD strip precedent). Fees + style stay settings-file-only. **Surfaces:** no snapshot/card/payload/CSV line changes; `BELOW_MIN_MOVE` renders identically (frozen policy-targetable token). **Tweaker — HARD CONSTRAINT 26:** `scoring.trade_costs.` PREFIX reject in `SettingsDiffApplier` + PromptBuilder rule 26; the retired key's exact-match reject removed with the key. **What-if:** the old key leaves the whitelist/launcher grid, `scoring.trade_costs.min_net_move_pct` joins (launcher row relabelled `Min net move % (after fees)`); fee/style keys NOT sweepable — the floor sweep IS the min-net sweep at fixed fees. **Fixtures:** new A40a–e (resolver composition across all three styles + default == 0.0008 to 1e-12 + derived props never serialise; defaults byte-identical through the REAL `Calculate()` against a cfg carrying the retired flat-key semantics, incl. a BELOW_MIN_MOVE case; knob turn moves the gate + the composed delta clears the eval re-walk epsilon; HC26 fence + retired-key C-6 rejection + what-if whitelist split; min_net overlay round-trips through `WhatIfSettings.BuildCellSettings`). A13d re-pointed to the new knob; A15f's exemplar re-pinned to the `trade_costs.` prefix. Acceptance: solution + AutoTweaker + WhatIfRunner + CeilingAudit + OrderCheck build **0/0** Release; A1–A39e unregressed; verify-gate `prepush` GATE PASSED. **Out of scope (named follow-ups, proposal §6):** eval net-EV rider (per-row fee drag in ATR = `round_trip_fee_pct × entry_price / ATR`, unconditional across all three outcome arms; slot = post-Aug-1 with the EV-dispersion column), optional display net-R:R rider, and the order-app relay (EV-aware chase budget + maker→taker SL-emergency delta; the bridge payload does NOT carry fees — no contract change). Local commit — trader tests + pushes. |

Spec-back: `absorption-geometry-rescale-spec-back.md`. **Mechanism:** three retired keys (`indicators.absorption.proximity_ticks / band_ticks / break_tol_ticks`) become applier-unresolvable (v53 pattern — deliberately NOT added to `RejectedPathFragments`, the v47-F1 snapshot-poisoning lesson); three new ATR-fraction keys populate at defaults **0.30 / 0.10 / 0.05** (≈$13.2 / $4.4 / $2.2 at ATR 44 — the design point of the 07-23 derivation). Resolved to absolute dollars **once per run** at the `SetAbsorptionLevels` carry site in `MainForm_Analysis.vb` (execution-resolution ATR by construction, since `r.ATR` carries the exec-resolution value); the `LevelAbsorptionTracker` keeps working in absolute dollars internally — `_proximityUsd` / `_bandUsd` / `_breakTolUsd` become tracker state refreshed at `SetLevels` alongside the four candidate-level fields; `FoldBook` / `FoldTrade` no longer read cfg tick fields (no `SignalEmitter.TickSize` multiplication anywhere in the tracker). Warmup: `r.ATR = 0` collapses the resolved distances to zero, the proximity gate cannot open, tracker stays IDLE by construction. **Floor rescale (all provisional against the widened shell):** `depletion_floor_usd` 25000 → 5000; `default.min_aggr_usd` 150000 → 20000; `absorb_ratio` 3.0 → 1.5; `max_pull_frac` 0.5 → 0.75 (the 07-23 derivation showed 0.5 vetoing 50–83% at tiny volumes where the lower-bound ratio is noise). **D8 conservation / visibility mask UNCHANGED** — the formulas follow whatever band is passed in, so widening the band flows through by construction (proposal §1). **Tweaker:** HC23 unchanged in *shape* (exact-match reject on `enabled`/`scoring_enabled`; prefix reject on `default.`/`sessions.`); comment text in `SettingsDiffApplier` + rule 23 in `PromptBuilder` updated to name the new `proximity_atr_frac`/`band_atr_frac`/`break_tol_atr_frac` tunables. **CSV:** header unchanged (v0.8 columns keep names + positions); Absorption* row VALUES start populating (the 07-23 derivation projects ~1–3 flagged rows per NY session-day at these anchors, ~0.5 per LONDON, ~0 per ASIA). **Retro-filter:** NOT POSSIBLE (proposal §2) — the tracker folds ~100 ms book snapshots + per-trade stream at fold time; neither is persisted, and episodes that never opened left no record. Re-collection under honest geometry is unavoidable (~1–1.5 weekday-weeks → §5 re-derivation → activation gates, ~mid-Aug). **POCO defaults ride this commit** (v33/v34 precedent): `AbsorptionSettings.ProximityAtrFrac/BandAtrFrac/BreakTolAtrFrac = 0.30/0.10/0.05`, `AbsorbRatio = 1.5`, `DepletionFloorUsd = 5000`, `MaxPullFrac = 0.75`, `AbsorptionDefaults.MinAggrUsd = 20000`; the three tick property/JsonPropertyName pairs REMOVED from the POCO. **Display-string parity:** strip-only surface (the ABS-tag TAPE-strip line), **no card/snapshot obligation** — the ABS tag renders the same shape from the same fields (v54 D6 precedent under the display-string parity rule). Fixtures: A31a–g re-pinned to fraction-resolved dollars via the extended `SetLevels(...,proximityUsd,bandUsd,breakTolUsd)` signature (fixed 6/2/1 USD to preserve the previous v54 test geometry byte-identical against unchanged books); A31g fence-test JSON literal + proposable check updated to the new key names. **New A31h — two-ATR scale invariance (V3):** same book-geometry logic replayed at ATR=44 (prox $13.2 / band $4.4 / brk $2.2) and ATR=88 (2×), verifying the tracker's resolution arithmetic gives identical classification. Acceptance: solution + AutoTweaker + WhatIfRunner + CeilingAudit + OrderCheck build **0/0** Release; A1–A39e unregressed + new A31h; verify-gate `prepush` GATE PASSED. **Post-ship watch (§12 addendum):** NY Absorption engagement rate on directional rows over the first weekday-week (target 3–8% band; if <1% after 5 weekday sessions → re-derive with looser default; if >12% → raise `absorb_ratio`); LONDON + ASIA on their own longer clocks per the derivation §4 note. Local commit — trader tests + pushes; coordinator review follows. |

| **v46** | 2026-06-30 | **Time-averaged OFI — P4 #4 (BUILD ONLY; ⚠ first scoring re-baseline upgrade, re-baseline deferred)** (settings v45→v46). Spec: `time-averaged-ofi-proposal.md` (APPROVED 2026-06-29, §10 all-recommended + the tweaker-exposure directive); spec-back: `time-averaged-ofi-spec-back.md`. On the live WS path the single-snapshot OFI is replaced by a **time-weighted average** of the top-book imbalance over the run window — a transient sweep/spoof can no longer flip the `OFIRatio`; the actionable signal is *sustained* imbalance. TWO new keys under `indicators.OFI`: `averaging_enabled` (true) + `avg_window_sec` (10). **Mechanism:** a host-agnostic feed-side rolling accumulator (`Core/OfiAccumulator.vb`) folds each ~100ms book update's weighted bid/ask imbalance into a **time-AWARE geometric (log-ratio) EMA** (`Ratio = exp(EMA(ln ratio))`; `alpha = 1 − exp(−dt/tau)`, `tau = avg_window_sec`, applied in log space; NOT fixed-alpha — the window must mean what it says under irregular arrivals) — **multiplicatively unbiased** for the symmetric bid/ask ratio, chosen over arithmetic per the **NY DIAG test** (2026-06-30, commit `eee6e4b`, reverted): arithmetic gave a **12:1** buy-dominant skew on a net-flat 5.1h session vs **1.4:1** geometric (AM≥GM bias — see `docs/ofi-geometric-construction-spec.md`). The weighted bid/ask volumes (`OFIBidVol`/`OFIAskVol`, display context) stay arithmetic. The folded scalar is the **same** sanity-bounded weighted ratio `CalcOFI` computes — the imbalance math + classification are now shared pure helpers (`IndicatorEngine.ComputeOfiImbalance` + `ClassifyOfiRatio`), so `CalcOFI` is **byte-identical** and the downstream OFISignal vote, the `_ofiHistory` momentum ring, and the breakdown/card render are unchanged in mechanism — **only the value of `r.OFIRatio` changes** (averaged vs snapshot). **⚠ Dataset boundary:** the `analysis_log.csv` `OFIRatio/OFISignal/OFIBidVol/OFIAskVol` columns keep their names but their *semantics* shift (averaged on the WS majority) — documented here + in `change_log`, **no header rotation** (same rule as v31/v36). The dominance-ratio **re-baseline (v47-ish) is a LATER data-gated pass** (firing-rate-match to the snapshot-OFI history, v40/v41 method), mirroring v36→v40 — this commit only produces the cleaner input, and that re-baseline (plus the `OFI.Momentum*` review) now runs on the **geometric** distribution, not the arithmetic one this row originally described. **Warmup/reset:** until the accumulator has ≥ `avg_window_sec` of fold coverage (+ a small min fold count) the run falls back to the snapshot `CalcOFI`; the accumulator resets on (re)connect so a stale pre-gap average can't bleed across — the fallback re-arms after every reconnect. **WS-only:** at `transport=rest` / per-run REST fallback there is one book/run → snapshot `CalcOFI` (today's path; never blocks). **Tweaker surface (trader directive):** `avg_window_sec` is **ON** the surface (it shapes the OFI signal — a real failure-rate lever) alongside `book_depth` + the dominance ratios; only `averaging_enabled` (the on/off feature flag, not a threshold) is **OFF** — exact-match rejected in `SettingsDiffApplier.Validate` + PromptBuilder **HARD CONSTRAINT 16** (siblings stay tunable). Coupling caveat: a manual `avg_window_sec` change should be followed by a dominance-ratio re-check (the ratios are re-derived *for* a given window). **Reversibility:** `averaging_enabled=false` reverts to snapshot OFI hot (the rollback) and is **byte-identical to v45** — proven by construction (the `If AveragingEnabled` short-circuit ⇒ `CalcOFI` is the only path) + harness **A20a/A20b**. **Code:** new `Core/OfiAccumulator.vb` (+ `OfiAverageSnapshot`); `IndicatorEngine.ComputeOfiImbalance`/`ClassifyOfiRatio` extracted from `CalcOFI`; `MarketState` owns the accumulator (`FoldOfi`/`ResetOfiAccumulator`/`GetOfiAverage` under its `SyncLock`); `DeribitWsFeed` folds on each book update + resets in `SeedAsync` (every connect); `RunAnalysisAsync` sources the OFI fields from the average on the WS-live path when warmed up. **Display-parity:** no new/removed/renamed rendered line — the OFI breakdown row + card binding (`MainForm_Render_Cards.vb`) render the same `r.OFIRatio` (value source unchanged), so no card-surface change. Acceptance: solution(Release) + AutoTweaker + OrderCheck build **0/0**; **A1–A19e unregressed** + **A20a–h** (CalcOFI refactor byte-identical, EMA steady-state + time-aware step, warmup gate, reset re-arm, tweaker reject `averaging_enabled` / accept `avg_window_sec`) + new **A20i** (geometric symmetry: alternating 2.0/0.5 converges to ~1.0, not the ~1.25 an arithmetic mean would give). No CSV-schema/eval-cache change (CSV v0.7, eval cache v4). **AMENDMENT (2026-07-01):** ratio construction switched arithmetic → geometric (log-ratio) per the NY DIAG test — spec `ofi-geometric-construction-spec.md`; folds INTO this v46 entry, **no version bump** (v47 stays reserved for the data-gated threshold re-baseline). Local commits only — trader tests + pushes. |
| **v45** | 2026-06-29 | **LIVE microstructure strip — P4 #3** (display/awareness only; **deliberately NOT a verdict**; **zero scoring/CSV/dataset impact, no re-baseline**; settings v44→v45). Spec: `live-microstructure-strip-proposal.md` (APPROVED 2026-06-25, §9 settled). ONE new top-level block `live_strip {enabled:true, refresh_sec:2, tape_window_sec:10}` (default flipped false→true after the trader cleared the §9 #1 visual checkpoint 2026-06-29). A continuously-updating one-line **TAPE** strip showing the fast streaming microstructure *between* full runs — last price bracketed by the **nearest structural level above & below** (`SH`/`SL`/`HVN↑`/`HVN↓` with signed Δ), **TFI**, **spread (bps)**, **top-book imbalance**, **tape speed** (`tr/s` + `$/s`) — at ~2s freshness. **Awareness, not a signal:** the §11 intent is explicit — the verdict stays a deliberate full-pipeline product; the strip shows raw microstructure *inputs*, visually distinct (neutral/dim, never the verdict colour ramp), so it informs without tempting a marginal entry. It **never** calls `Calculate()`, writes the CSV, or emits a direction/score — no schema change, no re-baseline. **Code:** new host-agnostic `LiveMicrostructureEvaluator.vb` (`Evaluate(state, lastRun, cfg) → MicrostructureSnapshot` — reuses the existing pure `CalcTFI`/`CalcSpread`/`CalcOFI` fns + **carried** last-run levels `LastSwingHigh/Low5m` + `VPFRNearestHvnAbove/Below`; identical methodology to the full run, only fresher; never throws, degenerate/empty buffer → safe blanks). Levels are **carried not recomputed** (5m swing / VPFR HVN are slow — refresh each full run); price/TFI/spread/imbalance/tape-speed recomputed live. WinForms host (`MainForm_LiveStrip.vb`) = a ~2s `System.Windows.Forms.Timer` (created at form load, disposed on close; **independent** of the auto-run + exit-guard + on-close timers), self-gating each tick (renders the readout only when `enabled` AND the WS feed is healthy+fresh; `transport=rest`/no `_marketState` → `TAPE · WS only`), a **thin full-width TAPE strip row directly under the verdict-header hero row**, and a **visible "TAPE" checkbox toggle** for `live_strip.enabled` (mirrors the SINGLE/REPEAT + INTERVAL/ON-CLOSE radio toggles; the data label hides when off so "disabled→hidden" (§7) holds while the toggle stays reachable (§10)). **Always-on when enabled** (NOT position-gated — useful flat watching a level for entry or in a hold). **OFF the auto-tweaker surface:** `"live_strip."` added to `SettingsDiffApplier.RejectedPathPrefixes` + PromptBuilder **HARD CONSTRAINT 15** (display preference, no failure-rate linkage — same class as `kelly.*`/`exit_guard.*`/`auto_run.*`). **Live status-bar element** like `BuildWsStatusSegment`/the exit-guard strip — NOT an RTF/snapshot/card surface, so **no card-binding obligation** (engine display-string parity rule, spec §6). Runtime toggle saves `bumpVersion:=False`; only the one-time key add bumps v44→v45. Acceptance: solution(Release) + AutoTweaker + OrderCheck build **0/0**; **A1–A18e unregressed** (engine path untouched) + new **A19a–e** evaluator fixtures (TFI/spread/imbalance correct, nearest-level above-vs-below bracketing, tape-speed window [recent counted / older excluded / lull → 0], empty buffer → blanks no throw); `transport=rest`/disabled → strip inert, verdict path byte-identical. No CSV/eval-cache change (CSV v0.7, eval cache v4). **§9 #1 checkpoint (overall look) CLEARED 2026-06-29** — trader reviewed the rendered strip → default flipped to `enabled:true` + the toggle made a visible "TAPE" checkbox (was a right-click context menu). **§9 #4 (both-levels) behaves as designed:** the strip shows the **nearest** carried level on **each** side (min of the >price candidates above / max of the <price candidates below). In a strong down-leg where price has broken below **all** carried structure (5m swing low/high + HVNs all sit above), only the nearest-*above* renders (e.g. `SL 59860 (+56)` — the broken swing low now overhead) and the below-bracket is genuinely empty until the next full run maps a level there; `SH` doesn't show because it's *further* above than `SL`, so `SL` wins the nearest-above slot. Not a bug — the strip surfaces "price is in free air below recent structure, nearest reference is the broken low overhead." Local commits only — trader tests + pushes. |

When on-close, the full analysis run fires the instant the **execution-resolution bar closes** (NY 1-min / Asia-London 3-min — v36) instead of on the fixed-interval timer — eliminating up-to-`interval` poll lag at the structural-breakout trader's decision moment (bar close). `RunAnalysisAsync` / `Calculate()` are **byte-identical**; only *when* a run fires moves — same observation logged at bar-close moments, no CSV schema change, no re-baseline. **On-close does NOT drop the forming bar** (that would be a re-baseline, out of scope) — the verdict at an on-close fire is exactly what a timer fire at that instant produces. **Code:** new host-agnostic `Core/BarCloseDetector.DetectBarRoll(state, execRes, lastSeenOpen)` (reads `MarketState.GetCandles(execRes)`; fires once when the forming-bar **open-time** (epoch-ms `Long`) advances; a multi-bar reconnect gap → **single** catch-up fire, not a burst; reuses `ExecutionResolution.ResolveResolution`). | **post-v41** | 2026-06-23 | **WebSocket reconnect storm-guard fix — count flaps, not still-down retries** (code-only; no settings bump; WS-feed robustness; `transport` stays `rest` → scoring/dataset untouched). Surfaced by the G1 reconnect drill (test 3): a 60s network kill left the feed showing `WS DOWN — reconnecting (300s backoff)` for ~5 min even after the network returned. Cause: `DeribitWsFeed.RunLoopAsync` storm guard = `>5 reconnects/10min`, but it counted retry-*attempts* — and exponential backoff (1→2→4→8→16→32s) racks up ~6 attempts in the first 60s of a *single* outage, tripping the 300s cooldown right as the network recovers. The app stayed functional throughout (REST fallback kept analysis running), so this was a recovery-latency flaw, not a correctness blocker. **Fix:** a `connectedThisCycle` flag — only a connect-then-drop (a genuine *flap*) is enqueued to the storm window; a still-down retry loop during one outage is not. So a single outage (however long) recovers within one ≤60s backoff cycle, while real flapping (repeated connect→drop) still cools down. `_reconnectCount` (status display) still counts every attempt. Builds 0/0 (solution Release + AutoTweaker + OrderCheck); harness A1–A15h unregressed (feed not harness-compiled → validated by the live drill re-run). |

Five items, all display/logging/hygiene; zero changes to Step 2–5 scoring votes, thresholds, or vetoes. Four commits `482c9bb`…`d3a0168`. **D1 (H4):** Kelly contract sizing was dimensionally wrong for the Deribit inverse contract — `riskPerContract = face × stopDistance` instead of `face × stopDistance / entryPrice` (~1e4× too large), so contracts were always 0 ("< 1 contract (stop too wide)" on every run). `CalcKellySizing` gains an `entryPriceUsd` param (both call sites pass `r.CurrentPrice`); at correct sizing leverage binds before the $ risk cap, so new `kelly.max_leverage` (default 5.0) caps contracts at `floor(account × maxLev / face)`, `final = min(risk-derived, leverage-derived)`, surfaced via new `VerdictResult.KellyLevCapped` and a KELLY notional/leverage line (`≈ $N · n.n× lev`, `[LEV CAPPED]` tag). Dead `"NEUTRAL"/"WAIT"` verdict guard deleted (strings retired ~20 versions ago). **D2 (S-1):** ATR stop/target distances were quadratic in volatility (`ATR × ATRScaleFactor × mult`); dropped `ATRScaleFactor` so distances are linear (`ATR × mult`) — reconciles the display with the eval pipeline, which already measured raw-ATR barriers. Sizing display relabelled from `× scale` (CurrATR/AvgATR) to `size ×N` (ATRRef/ATR = the profile's AvgATR/CurrATR factor); DYNAMIC NORMS prefix `ATR scale` → `ATR ratio`. `r.ATRSizeMultiplier` (CSV `ATRMultiplier`) keeps its meaning. **D3 (S-2):** `VPFR-lite` added to the context-tag structural set (was counted toward neither struct nor flow, biasing toward STRUCTURALLY_WEAK / FLOW_UNCONFIRMED). **D4 (S-4):** auto-run start/stop no longer churns settings.json — `StopAutoRun` drops its `Save`; `StartAutoRun` saves only on a genuine interval change; dead `auto_run.enabled` key + `Enabled` POCO removed. **D5 (S-6):** new host-agnostic `IndicatorEngine.IsFresh` (last bar older than 2× resolution → stale) wired into the 1m/5m skip-gate; 15m cache tolerates staleness by design. **settings v31 → v32:** `kelly.max_leverage` added, `auto_run.enabled` removed; CSV stays v0.6, no eval-cache change. Local-only acceptance harness extended: A10 (Kelly lev-cap), A11 (freshness), A12 (linear levels) — all pass alongside A1–A9. |
| **post-v31** | 2026-06-11 | CSV schema v0.5 → v0.6 (no settings.json bump). Columns 89-93 added: MicroCVDEarly/Mid/Late (net USD deltas — negative values valid), MicroCVDMomentum, MicroCVDSignal. MicroCVD was never logged to the CSV, so the accel-threshold re-baseline (§12 WATCHING) would have had no CSV data to sweep — added immediately post-reset while the v0.5 file held only ~5 rows (rotated to `analysis_log.csv.v0.5.bak`; rotation bak-name literals updated v0.4 → v0.5). Verified live: 93/93 header/row alignment, chronological E/M/L values in the first logged row. All `analysis_log.csv` readers are header-name-based since F9, so the appended columns are transparent to them. |
| **v31** | 2026-06-11 | **Engine correctness pass** — one bundled fix event at a single reset boundary. Spec: `engine-correctness-pass-proposal.md`; audit: `fable5-audit-report.md`. Five sequential commits, all behaviour-changing fixes before the data reset. **F1** (C1/C2/C3, CRITICAL): `GetRecentTradesAsync` returns chronological ASCENDING (request stays `sorting=desc`; list reversed before return — preserves intra-ms ordering); TFI + MicroCVD windows take from the END via new `LastN` helper; CVD slope and MicroCVD accel/decel polarity become chronologically truthful (hold/exit cues inherit the fix); last-transacted price reads the last element. **F2** (H1): DynamicNorms volume (100) + VWAP-dev (50) baselines sample the most recent completed bars, mirroring `ComputeATRRef`. **F3** (H2): direction-aware MTF gate — `CalcMTFGate` emits per-side flags (`MTFGatePassLong/Short`) + direction-free details; the 1m pre-scoring proposal is deleted; Step 4b consults the dominant side's flag and composes the final reason (three locked formats) onto `VerdictResult.MTFGateReason`/`MTFGateBlocked`, which every consumer renders; regime-veto early returns now append the MTF breakdown row. **F4** (H3): Step 5 walks only the dominant side's tiers; ties carry no direction → NO TRADE; `AppendLean` long-bias tie removed, explicit `[TIE]` tag. **F5** (M1): OBV change normalised by mean per-bar volume (units: average-bar-volumes of drift); `indicators.OBV.trend_gate` 0.001 → **10.0 (seeded, WATCHING)**. **F6** (M2): Donchian channel spans the prior `period` bars (current bar excluded) — full breakouts genuinely reachable. **F7** (S-5): Step 3 funding boost capped at regimeMax. **F8** (S-6): TRANSITIONAL ADX penalty first arm covers [0, mid) — grace-bar ADX < 20 now gets the full penalty. **F9** (M7): `LivePerformanceTracker` CSV backfill parses by header name (was fixed indices — required by the schema shift). **CSV v0.4 → v0.5**: gate columns now `MTFGatePassLong`, `MTFGatePassShort`, `MTFGateReason` (final composed string); same-name columns `CVDSlope`/`MicroCVD*`/`VolumeRatio`/`OBV*`/`DonchianSignal` change semantics — the header is the schema marker. Acceptance harness `verify/ordercheck/` recreated glob-safely (root `.vbproj` `Compile Remove verify/**`); fixtures A1–A9 all pass. **Data reset executed** (pre-authorised 2026-06-10): `analysis_log.csv`, `analysis_eval_cache.csv`, tweaker `state.json`, picked-cell history, `settings_snapshots/`, output dump archived to `data-archive/pre-orderfix-20260611/` (gitignored); `ohlc_1m_cache.csv` kept (raw input, uncontaminated). Auto-tweaker stays held until ≥1 full clean window + supervised first fire. Expect on day one: hold/exit cues track the tape, wrong-side WEAKs gone, MTF blocks redistribute both ways, more NO TRADE on provisional thresholds — different ≠ regression; judge against the tape. |

Labelled INTERMEDIATE not MACRO because 5m EMA(200) is ~3.3 hours of data — true macro context (daily timeframe) needs a separate spec for Deribit daily candle fetch. |
| **post-v30** | 2026-05-18 | `analysis/FailureRateMatrix.vb` dual recommendation per tier — `IsRecommended` (lowest CI width, auto-tweaker view) preserved; new `IsMostProfitable` flag (lowest failure rate with n ≥ MinSamplesPerCell, trader view). `MarkdownReportWriter` renders both: ★ for IsRecommended, ◆ for IsMostProfitable, ★◆ when same cell. §1 headline, §2 matrix, §3 (with new legend), §8 hold-window stats, and CSV summary all dual-view. Surfaced because the original picker minimised CI width, which favours extreme p values — for high-failure tiers (e.g. STRONG_LONG 69% recommended cell) the "recommended" cell was the worst-performing one, confusing for human readers. Auto-tweaker continues to read `IsRecommended` (correct for the most-precise-estimate consumer). |
| **post-v30** | 2026-05-18 | `analysis/DeribitOhlcFetcher.vb` chunked fetch — closes STRONG_SHORT cell-eligibility starvation. Surfaced in 2026-05-17 Analysis Report: STRONG_SHORT showed n=0 in every failure-matrix cell despite 47 STRONG SHORT verdicts in CSV. Root cause: `FetchOhlcRange` made a single `DeribitClient.GetCandlesAsync` call. Deribit caps responses at ~5000 bars per call, so CSV spans >5000 minutes lost the oldest portion of OHLC. All 47 STRONG SHORTs clustered in the first 2 hours of CSV (2026-05-13 13:56–16:11 UTC+8) — all silently excluded from the matrix. Fix: loop in `CHUNK_MINUTES=5000` segments with `MAX_CHUNKS=20` safety cap, mid-range failure aborts the whole fetch (avoids silently-partial OHLC maps). Same pattern as `LivePerformanceTracker.FetchGapChunked` (v27). |

## F. Historical / Superseded Spec Docs

The following spec files in `/docs` are kept for historical reference. They do NOT reflect current engine state — the corresponding feature has been re-specced or superseded:

- `bbw-scoring-proposal.md` / `bbw-scoring-response.md` — original BBW scoring design, pre-v0.41
- `dual-scoring-fix-proposal.md` / `dual-scoring-fix-response.md` — legacy dual-scoring approach, pre-v0.30
- `failure-definition-proposal.md` — original ATR forward-return failure definition. **Superseded** by `failure-definition-v2-proposal.md` (2026-05-07 barrier-hit semantics).

For all currently-shipped specs, see `docs/DeribitIndicatorProject.md` §3 Docs table.

## G. Resolved WATCHING / Calibration Items (moved from §12, 2026-07-02 housekeeping)

- **Post-correctness-pass re-baseline (OBV/CVD/MicroCVD/session-volume seed)** — RESOLVED by v33 (partial, NY-only) + v34 (full, CalibrationReport READY at 975 rows). OBV `trend_gate` 10→18, CVD `slope_pct_of_value` 0.01→0.05→0.10, MicroCVD `accel_threshold_dynamic_pct` 0.03→0.30, `session_volume` ASIA 0.80/0.85→1.10/1.05; volume clamps + Donchian quartile re-confirmed. Specs: `clean-data-rebaseline-v33-proposal.md` / `-v34-proposal.md`. (Residual weekday-ASIA re-verify stayed in §12.)
- **v36 Phase-2 ROC re-baseline (the 2.1× proxy)** — RESOLVED by v40 + v41 (workstream B, 2026-06-20/22): firing-rate-matched to NY-1m on the weekday 3-min book. Magnitude went per-session (`session_volume.sessions[].roc_magnitude_threshold` — ASIA 0.17, LONDON 0.11; `resolution_profiles["3"]` keeps 0.21 as fallback); slope stayed shared (`resolution_profiles["3"].roc_slope_delta_threshold` 0.105→0.06). Spec: `asia-london-roc-rebaseline-proposal.md`.
- **3-min hold-window recalibration (offline matrix + eval horizon)** — IMPLEMENTED + VALIDATED + CLOSED 2026-06-24: resolution-scaled windows `{5,10,15}×execRes` → 3-min `{15,30,45}` (`3d473a1`), `LivePerformanceTracker` horizon scaled via `EvalHorizonMinutes(res)` (`1825fad`); plateau confirmed on the 5,275-row post-soak book (failure flattens at 30m; 45m adds zero new successes). Specs: `three-min-hold-window-recalibration-proposal.md` / `-spec-back.md`.
- **OI × CVD gate tuning** — RESOLVED-KEEP 2026-07-03 (W1 signal-health audit R5), archived on the v50 retune ship: CONFLICT 38.9% vs CONFIRMED 55.6% SUCC (n=18/27), gradient in the designed direction at both ends; engages on 2.9% of runs. No change to `oi_cvd` settings. Evidence: `signal-health-audit-2026-07-03.md`.
- **Bid-ask spread threshold (revival question)** — RESOLVED-KEEP 2026-07-03 (W1 audit R3/F4), archived on the v50 retune ship: the revival premise is refuted — one-tick book on both transports (WS p50–p95 0.08–0.09 bps, p99 0.59; >5 bps on 0.1% of runs); 5.0 kept as an inert tail-guard; **A1 spread-momentum REJECTED with evidence** (no variance to ride). Evidence: `signal-health-audit-2026-07-03.md`.

**Second batch moved 2026-08-02** (context-budget housekeeping) — §12 rows whose status cell had reached a terminal value (`Resolved` / `Superseded`). Live rows, including everything marked `Blocked`, `Medium` or `Low`, stayed in §12. Verbatim, nothing rewritten.

| AtrTargetMultiplier | ✅ RESOLVED 2026-07-06 (**v51 B4b placed geometry**): the reach-target calibration ran as the placed-geometry §6 derivation — fallback target 2.0 → **1.75** global (NY reach 56.8%, the 55–60% design point) + LONDON 2.0 / ASIA 1.25 session overrides; structure is now the PRIMARY placed-level source (target ladder + DG1 stop). Successor watch: the v51 §12 post-ship items (realized reach-rate, STOP_CLAMPED frequency, BELOW_MIN_MOVE, LONDON structural-target inversion). | Resolved (v51) |
| OFI ratio | Buy/Sell dominant 2.0/0.5. **Re-homed → the v48 dominance re-baseline** (roadmap W1; firing-rate-match on the geometric distribution, `time-averaged-ofi-spec-back.md` §5) — data-gated, collection running since 2026-07-02. | Superseded by v48 |
| Swing pivot wing/lookback | `pivot_wing_5m=3` / `lookback_bars_5m=30`. **✅ CLOSED 2026-07-31 — no change, evidenced (JOB 2 decision D-D).** Widening the wing 3→5 costs **14.3pp of placeability** (45.5% → 31.2%) — the trade fails on that column alone. Lookback is **not** the constraint at the live wing: median pivot age **8 bars** against a lookback of 30, and lb45 moves placeability **+0.1pp**. Caveats recorded so they are not re-cited wrongly: the packet's "7.5pp of durability" framing is **withdrawn** — the crossing column is direction-blind (for a long, a crossed swing high is the *target being reached*), so it is context only and the trade is **not** 2-for-1; and lookback *is* binding at w5 (both-found 92.8% → 98.9% at lb45), just not at wing 3. The outcome-joined false-positive rate remains blocked — it needs trades-covered replay. Evidence: [`candle-store-derivations-2026-07-31.md`](candle-store-derivations-2026-07-31.md) §4, read in [`job2-read-2026-07-31.md`](job2-read-2026-07-31.md) §3. | Resolved (no change) |
| **3-min weekday-ASIA `session_volume` re-verify (v34 confound + v36 resolution)** | v34 set ASIA `high/mid_mult` to 1.10/1.05 on a sample that was **100% Saturday** (weekend); the NY baseline was 100% weekday, so the comparison is weekday/weekend-confounded. **Post-v36, ASIA runs on 3-min**, and `session_volume` stays 1-min-calibrated + out of the resolution profile (VolumeRatio ≈ scale-invariant; hand-off §6.2). So this re-verify **becomes the 3-min weekday re-verify**: once ≥50 weekday-Asia **3-min** rows exist, recompute ASIA trade-rate / RANGE_BOUND % / VolumeRatio tail on weekday-only 3-min Asia rows; if weekday Asia is materially calmer than the Saturday sample, dial 1.10/1.05 back toward neutral (1.00/1.00). Do it **by hand** (NOT the auto-tweaker — no day-of-week concept; resolution-blind until Phase 2). **Bundles the offline Deribit volume re-fetch** (re-anchors OBV's `|obvChange|` median on multi-session data + the ASIA/LONDON clamp-binding check + ASIA fire-rate). Spec: `clean-data-rebaseline-v34-proposal.md` §3.1 + spec-back §7; `session-timeframe-resolution-implementer-handoff.md` §6.2. **✅ RESOLVED 2026-07-22 (v58) — closed here 2026-07-31 (JOB 2 decision D-E, doc drift).** The read ran (`asia-session-volume-reverify-2026-07-22.md`): weekday Asia is materially calmer than the Saturday sample — trade-rate 29.4% vs 63.1% — and the recommendation to dial ASIA back to neutral **1.00/1.00** was ticked and **shipped as v58** (`bd31a1a`); `settings.json` now reads ASIA 1.00/1.00. `roadmap.md` (2026-07-22) and `backlog-dependency-map.md` both recorded it closed on the day; **this row was the last stale copy**, and because §12 is the source other docs inherit from, anyone reading it first inherited the error. The **offline volume re-fetch** this row bundled now also exists (the 6-month store), and the derivations it unblocked ran on 2026-07-31 — see `candle-store-derivations-2026-07-31.md`. | Resolved (v58) |

## H. Roadmap — spent planning sections (moved 2026-08-07)

**Provenance.** `roadmap.md` §2 had become an append-only log of twelve dated update paragraphs running 2026-07-02 → 2026-07-22 while the tree ran to v65; its §4 (the Fable window schedule) and §5 (the month execution order) were both fully worked through. On 2026-08-07 §2 was **replaced** by a verified snapshot plus a compressed shipped ledger, and §4/§5 by a forward-looking "what runs next" section.

**All three are reproduced below VERBATIM** — the only edit is demoting their `##` headers to `###` so they nest under this section. They are kept because the roadmap is the history future orchestrators reference: the compressed ledger records *what* shipped, and these records *why it was sequenced that way and what was measured at the time* — the correlation-gate coefficients, the 13-column v0.8 CSV manifest, the ruling that deferred the CLI port, the F1 gate that still binds Kelly CAL.

**Nothing here is current state.** For that: [`trader-tick-queue.md`](trader-tick-queue.md) §0a. For dependencies: [`backlog-dependency-map.md`](backlog-dependency-map.md). This section is a record of decisions as they were made.

### Root

| File | Purpose |
|---|---|
| `DeribitClient.vb` | All Deribit REST calls incl. 15m candles, recentTrades. `ExecuteWithRetry` wraps every public `GetXxxAsync`. |
| `DynamicNorms.vb` | ATR/Vol/VWAP norm computation; session-aware volume threshold adjustment via `ApplySessionVolume`. |
| `AnalysisLogger.vb` | CSV logging + CalibrationReport. v0.4 schema rotates old logs on header mismatch. |
| `AnalysisOutputDump.vb` | Persistent markdown record of full rendered analysis text per run (v25; v30 adds optional `perfStripLine` param). |
| `LivePerformanceTracker.vb` | Live perf-strip eval cache + window aggregator. `InitialiseAsync` Step 1.5 gap-fills OHLC (v27). v2 eval schema with `TargetEverHit` (v28). |
| `OhlcCache.vb` | Rolling 7-day 1m OHLC cache. Load / Append / WriteAll / RollingTrim / NewestBarTime. |
| `OiSnapshot.vb` | OI ring-buffer helper. |
| `AutoRunTimer.vb` | `IAutoRunTimer` interface + `WinFormsAutoRunTimer` impl. Linux CLI variant planned. |
| `Program.vb` | Entry point. |
| `settings.json` | v30 — all tunable parameters. See §6 for the operational pointer; `change_log` array inside the file is the source of truth for version history. |
| `MainForm.Designer.vb` | Auto-generated WinForms designer (do not edit). |
| `MainForm.resx` | Form resources. |

### Core/

| File | Purpose |
|---|---|
| `Core/ScoringEngine_Types.vb` | SignalBreakdownItem, VerdictResult (incl. `AdjustedLongTarget`/`AdjustedShortTarget`, `TargetCapReasonLong/Short` split — B1 fix 2026-05-12, `VerdictContext`, Kelly fields, `Timestamp`, `OiCvdOutcome`), PositionState, SignalCategory, ScoreState. |
| `Core/ScoringEngine_Helpers.vb` | `RegimeMaxScore` (cfg `scoring.regime_max_score`), `Threshold`, `TierFloor` (cfg `scoring.tier_floor`), `AddFull`, `HasCrossConfirm`, `BuildNote`, `CalcHoldStatus` (Layer 1 microstructure / **Layer 1.5 structural-break exit** / Layer 2 momentum break (ROC crosses 0) then OBV divergence / Layer 3 RSI/ROC). |
| `Core/ScoringEngine_Calculate_Scoring.vb` | `AppendLean()`, `CalcVerdictContext()` (returns ALIGNED on NO TRADE per v30 F11; FLOW_UNCONFIRMED / MOMENTUM_FADING / STRUCTURALLY_WEAK / CONFIRMED / ALIGNED), `RunScoringPipeline()` Steps 2 / Pass 2 / Pass 2b / Pass 2c / 3 / 3b. |
| `Core/ScoringEngine_Calculate_Verdict.vb` | `Calculate()` entry point. Step 4 regime veto + TRANSITIONAL ADX penalty. Step 4b MTF gate veto. Step 5 verdict. **Step 5b 3-tier target cap** (swing → nearest HVN → POC). |
| `Core/ScoringEngine_Kelly.vb` | `CalcKellySizing()` — display-only, called from `MainForm_Render_Header` not from `Calculate()`. Zero scoring impact. |
| `Core/IndicatorResults.vb` | IndicatorResults struct. All indicator output fields incl. `FundingMomentum`, `SpreadBps`, `OFIMomentum`, VPFR-v2 fields, swing pivot fields, `TrendStructure5m`, `BestPivotByVolume5m`. |
| `Core/Indicators_Momentum.vb` | CalcDMI, CalcATR, CalcEMA, CalcRSI, CalcRSISeries, **CalcRSIDivergence** (v21 semantic rewrite), CalcROCSeries, CalcVolumeSMA. |
| `Core/Indicators_Volatility.vb` | CalcVWAP (dual-session), CalcVWAPBands, CalcBBW, CalcTTMSqueeze. |
| `Core/Indicators_OrderFlow.vb` | CalcOFI, CalcOFIMomentum, CalcLiquidations, CalcCVD, CalcMicroCVD (dynamic accelThreshold), CalcTFI, **CalcFundingMomentum + AppendFundingSample** (v53 time-anchored window + 30-min ring eviction). |
| `Core/Indicators_Structure.vb` | CalcDonchian, CalcOBV, CalcVPFRLite v2 (VAH/VAL + nearest HVN/LVN), **CalcSwingPivots** (5m primary + 15m context), **ClassifyTrendStructure** (HH/HL/LH/LL D1), CalcMTFGate. |
| `Core/Settings/EngineSettings.vb` | POCO contract for `settings.json`. All sub-settings classes. |
| `Core/Settings/SettingsLoader.vb` | JSON deserialisation. `SettingsLoader.Current` singleton. `FileSystemWatcher` hot-reload. `Save(cfg, changeNote)` writes back with auto change_log entry. |

### UI/

| File | Purpose |
|---|---|
| `UI/MainForm_Layout.vb` | Shared fields, constructor, status-bar cascade, NUD centring. Owns `_fundingHistory`, `_ofiHistory`, six perf-strip labels + `lblPerfMode` (`[B]`/`[T]` indicator), `_metricMode`, `_perfContextMenu`. |
| `UI/MainForm_AutoRun.vb` | Auto-run timer lifecycle. |
| `UI/MainForm_Analysis.vb` | `RunAnalysisAsync()` — full data fetch + indicator + scoring pipeline. Raises `AnalysisCompleted` event. |
| `UI/MainForm_Render_Header.vb` | RTF helpers, CalibrationReport/log helpers, `RenderOutputHeader()`: VERDICT / CONTEXT / CONFIDENCE / SCORE / TIME / LAST PRICE / HOLD\EXIT (gated on `posState ≠ None`) / ATR levels / Long+Short structural rows / KELLY block. v30 `FormatRR` helper, `capNoiseFloor` for sub-tick CAPPED suppression. |
| `UI/MainForm_Render_Sections.vb` | `RenderOutput()` entry point, all indicator sections, FUNDING section (v30 negative-zero clamp), signal breakdown table, verdict label update, perf-strip line composition for output dump. |
| `UI/OutputDumpSettingsForm.vb` | Non-modal dialog for output dump settings (Enabled toggle, max-runs, clear/save). |
| `UI/TweakSettingsForm.vb` | Non-modal Auto-Tweaker settings dialog. v29 adds MinTier textbox + auto-track-WindowSize logic. |
| `UI/RoundStatsForm.vb` | Non-modal RichTextBox showing per-tier accuracy via `RoundStatsBuilder`. |
| `UI/AnalysisReportForm.vb` | Non-modal viewer for offline analysis markdown report. |

The real vote rate is already **43.4% / 46.3%**, so the ladder would push it to ~34–36%, the wrong way. Re-derive against the post-gate vote rate; take the 3m value off the measured 1.45, not the 40÷25 grid quotient. **⚠ live scoring change ⇒ own spec + dataset boundary** (D-A, direction-only). | Medium (re-derive the anchor) |

| **Auto-tweaker tier-eligibility floor vs the v35 book (MinTier mismatch)** | v29's `min_tier_eligible_rows` default auto-scales as `max(15, ceil(WindowSize×0.5))`, assuming ~50% of window rows are tier-eligible (STRONG/standard LONG/SHORT only — WEAK and NO TRADE are excluded, `AutoTweakerCore.vb` lines 214-218). The conservative post-v35 engine delivers far less: NY measured **~23% actionable-directional** (128/551 NY rows, 2026-06-15; remaining 37% WEAK + 30% NO TRADE both excluded). So the shipped 30-row window / MinTier 15 can **never** clear the floor on the live book — a 30-row window averages ~7 eligible vs the 15 needed, so every block trips `SKIPPED_INSUFFICIENT_TIER` and burns the rows without firing. **Interim (2026-06-15):** bumped `window_size_verdicts` 30→75 in `tweaker_config.json` for the supervised first fire (keeps MinTier 15 statistically meaningful at ~17 eligible/window). **Proper fix:** recalibrate the window/MinTier pair against the real post-v35 directional rate, per session (NY ~23%; Asia/London likely lower → may need session-specific windowing — dovetails with the session+resolution filtering the tweaker needs before tuning beyond NY/1-min anyway). | Medium |

| **Auto-tweaker resolution-awareness (Phase-2 precondition)** | The tweaker's fixed-window slicer walks disjoint **chronological** row slices and is **resolution-blind** (compounds the existing session-blind gap). Before it tunes on any post-v36 data it MUST filter the failure-rate matrix + CSV rows by `(session × resolution)` so it never pools 3-min Asia/London with 1-min NY. The `ExecResolution` CSV stamp (v0.7) makes this possible; `execution_resolution` is already on HARD CONSTRAINT 11's exclusion list (it can never *propose* a resolution change). **Hard ordering:** the v35 NY/1-min supervised first-fire dry-run must consume the clean 1-min history **before** v36 ships 3-min rows (once 3-min rows interleave, no later window isolates a pure-1-min population). Spec the `(session × resolution)` filter as a Phase-2 item before any un-gated fire on mixed data. **Phase-2a addressed (2026-06-17, `auto-tweaker-session-resolution-filter-implementer-handoff.md`):** load-time `(session × resolution)` population filter in `AutoTweakerCore` (consumes `CsvRow.ExecResolution`, derives session via the shared `MatchSessionBucket`; initial population NY×1), state re-seed on filter change (`TweakerState.PopulationFilterKey`), and a **code-level** off-surface reject in `SettingsDiffApplier.Validate` (`kelly.*` / `resolution_profiles.*` prefixes + `scoring.min_tradeable_move_pct` exact — hardens the previously prompt-only HARD CONSTRAINT 11). Harness A15a–A15g + A1–A14 green. **Carry-forward (Phase-2b):** per-population `LastEvaluatedRowIndex`/round-robin, per-population `WindowSize`/`MinTier`/threshold, picked-cell/round-history segregation by population, the schema-home decision for session-specific tuned values, and the wholesale-revert-vs-manual-`resolution_profiles["3"]` interaction (a revert restores `resolution_profiles` from the snapshot — supervised/`auto_commit`-gated, flagged not fixed). The **manual `resolution_profiles["3"]` re-baseline** (the Asia/London accuracy fix) is a separate data-gated settings pass, NOT the tweaker. Source: hand-off §6.1/§6.3 + spec-back §4. | Medium (Phase-2a done; Phase-2b open) |

Source: `docs/DeribitIndicatorProject.md` lines 396-396 at git tag `doc-trim-2026-09-14-pre` - 6557 B - SHA-256 `552a99112d58b9d8d2b5e5b32a0d54d1b47675cbcdbb3380b67c8adc60216f06`.

| **Venue-status instrument — `Core/VenueStatusLog.vb` + `DeribitClient` helper + `collector.ps1` + fixtures A74a–A75b** (display/observation only, settings-untouched) | 2026-09-11 | **Spec: [`venue-status-instrument-spec.md`](venue-status-instrument-spec.md). D-1 ruled (d) 2026-09-11 by the trader — a new private `GetStringOrRecordAsync` helper replaces all 6 `_http.GetStringAsync(url)` call sites in `DeribitClient.vb` (lines 145/199/244/263/280/314 post-edit), calling `GetAsync` instead so the status code AND body are both available. New `Core/VenueStatusLog.vb` mirrors `WsHealthLog.vb`: transition-only, never-throws, host-agnostic, append-only sidecar at `AppDomain.BaseDirectory + "venue_status.log"`.** Three shapes: A — any 5xx → LOG `VENUE_<code>` then throw the same `HttpRequestException` `GetStringAsync` throws (ExecuteWithRetry control flow untouched); B — HTTP 200 carrying a JSON-RPC error object (`"error"` key at root) → LOG `VENUE_RPC_<rpc-code>` (e.g. `VENUE_RPC_11051`) or `VENUE_RPC_UNKNOWN` (D-3 — error object with no readable code), return body UNCHANGED (`RecordVenueIfNeeded` always ends `Return body`); Source: `docs/DeribitIndicatorProject.md` lines 397-397 at git tag `doc-trim-2026-09-14-pre` - 4298 B - SHA-256 `789c0495e09f2da222346fafcc454862ef5bc7f559263cead12b45c4d762a1a6`.

| **Atomic writes — the partial primitive swapped for a TOTAL one at all SIX call sites** (settings-untouched) | 2026-09-10 | **`File.Replace` THROWS when the destination does not exist, which is exactly the first-write case, so every site had to carry an `If File.Exists` guard alongside it. `File.Move(..., overwrite:=True)` is TOTAL — it works whether or not the destination exists.** ⭐ **The framing that makes this worth doing, and it is not tidiness: a guard is what you OWE when the primitive is PARTIAL. Choosing a total primitive REMOVES the obligation rather than satisfying it, so the hazard class is deleted instead of watched.** ⛔⛔ **THE ROW THAT SPECIFIED THIS SAID "5 sites" AND THERE ARE SIX — and the count was the finding.** Three documents carried the undercount (`trader-tick-queue.md` §2, `roadmap.md`, `backlog-dependency-map.md`, all corrected 2026-09-10). **The uncounted site is `OhlcCache.vb` RollingTrim — precisely the one the spec row itself flagged as *"safe only via a distant early-out"*, so an implementer sizing at five would have made five changes and missed the least safe one.** **The six sites:** `Core/Settings/SettingsLoader.vb` `AtomicWriteAllText` · `Core/SignalEmitter.vb` `TryWrite` · `OhlcCache.vb` `WriteAll` · `OhlcCache.vb` `RollingTrim` · `tools/AutoTweaker/SettingsDiffApplier.vb` `AtomicWriteAllText` · `tools/AutoTweaker/TweakerState.vb` `Save`. ⭐ **ATOMICITY IS PRESERVED, and the reason is load-bearing: every site writes `path & ".tmp"`, a SIBLING, so source and destination are always on the same volume — the condition Win32 `MoveFileEx` needs to replace atomically rather than copy-then-delete.** ⚠ **ONE deliberate behaviour change, verified inert rather than assumed: `File.Replace` preserved the DESTINATION's ACLs and creation time; `File.Move` carries the SOURCE's. The `.tmp` is created in the same directory so it inherits the same ACLs the destination had, and nothing in the repo reads `CreationTime` (grepped 2026-09-10, zero hits). It would matter only for a file carrying EXPLICIT, non-inherited ACLs — none of the six does.** ⛔ **A comment at `OhlcCache.vb` RollingTrim instructed a future reader to ADD THE GUARD if the distant early-out ever moved. That instruction is now WRONG — with a total primitive the guard would be dead code — so it is rewritten, with the original kept as superseded text because it explains why that site once looked inconsistent with its sibling.** ⛔ **ENGINE DISPLAY-STRING PARITY RULE: DOES NOT FIRE.** No line emitted by `BuildPlaintextSnapshot` or bound in `MainForm_Render_Cards.vb` changes; these are file writes. **Confirmed by the gate, not asserted: *"no snapshot/card drift detected"*.** **Fixtures A72a–A72c**, asserting through `SignalEmitter.TryWrite` because it is Public, is one of the six swapped sites, and its own doc comment previously called the guard *"LOAD-BEARING"*. ⭐ **MUTATION RUN, actual output pasted rather than predicted — revert `TryWrite` to a bare `File.Replace`: `A72a` FAILS (`absentBefore=True returned=False exists=False`), `A72b` PASSES (it only exercises the existing-destination branch), `A72c` FAILS.** ⚠⚠ **THE MUTATION FOUND A DEFECT IN THE FIXTURE ITSELF: `A72c`'s first draft called `ReadAllText` unguarded, so under the mutation it ABORTED THE HARNESS with `FileNotFoundException` instead of failing — which would have hidden every fixture after it. Now guarded; it fails cleanly with `content=<<absent>>`. A fixture that throws instead of failing is a worse defect than the one it was written to catch.** ⚠ **Also corrected: the fixture comment's own prediction that `A72c` would PASS under the mutation was wrong, written from reasoning rather than from a run.** ⚠ **Coverage boundary, stated: the two `tools/AutoTweaker` copies live in a project the harness does not link, and `SettingsLoader.AtomicWriteAllText` is `Private` — those three are covered by review, not by a fixture. `AtomicWriteAllText` now exists in THREE hand-kept copies; a comment at each names the other two.** Harness **349 → 352**, ALL PASS. Solution-wide Release `-t:Rebuild` 0 errors 0 warnings. `verify-gate.ps1 -Mode local-fast` GATE PASSED. `settings.json` untouched at **v68** — no config key added or changed. |

NO `settings.json` key, NO version bump — `S-4` dates to v26 but needs neither (`git diff --stat -- settings.json` is empty). **Mechanism:** `EvalCacheEntry` and the (now `Friend`) `LogRow` both gain `InstanceId As String` / `SignalId As Long?`, deliberately **not** defaulted to a non-null value — an absent identity must stay distinguishable from a real one, or the `D-3` (a) fallback cannot tell which rows are legacy. `ParseAnalysisLog` reads both **by name from `colIdx`**, never by position (today's CSV column 110/111 is what the file happens to be, not a contract) and is absent-tolerant for pre-#5-v0.8 books. The v6→v7 migration is a **plain re-stamp** — `IsPreV7Schema` (mirrors `IsPreV5Schema`/`IsPreV6Schema`, gated on the schema comment's new "identity pair" marker, captured **before** any rewrite alongside the file's five other pre-existing probes) triggers one `WriteEvalCache` call that adds the two columns and touches NOTHING else, because `LoadEvalCache`/`FormatEvalEntry` already round-trip every other field unchanged — no re-walk exists to write, unlike the v5→v6 no-data sweep it sits beside. **The dedup key itself:** new `KeyFor` (three overloads) returns `"ID\|"&instanceId&"\|"&signalId` when both are present, else falls back to `"TS\|"&Timestamp` — the `"ID\|"`/`"TS\|"` prefixes keep the two identity spaces provably disjoint. **The loop fix:** the old inline Step-3 backfill loop is extracted into `Friend Shared Function BackfillNewEntries(logRows, existingCache, cfg, nowUtc)` — pure, no module-state I/O, mirroring `AggregateRange`/`EvaluateEntry`/`ReclassifyWindowExpiredForNoData`'s existing Friend-pure pattern in this file — and the identity-key set (kept named `existingTs`, now holding composite keys, because that is the literal string the spec's own `AC-5` handle greps for) is updated **inside** the loop as each row is admitted. `BuildEntry` and `BuildLiveEntry` thread the identity through; `BuildLiveEntry` reads `ProcessIdentity.InstanceId`/`CurrentSignalId` directly — verified safe by reading `UI/MainForm_Analysis.vb:634` and `:678`, where `NextSignalId()` ticks strictly before `LivePerformanceTracker.UpdateAsync` runs, so `CurrentSignalId` always reflects the run under way, never the prior one. **Fixtures `A69a`–`A69d`**, all newly written (family `A69` was free at the spec's baseline `a6cfb8d`): `A69a` reproduces the exact pre-fix asymmetry synthetically (cold total 2 / warm total 1 under a reverted `D-1` (b)) and PASSES at 1/1 post-fix; `A69b` a three-row batch with two colliding rows admits exactly two; `A69c` — ⭐ **the fixture that actually distinguishes `D-2` (b) from both wrong keys at once** — two rows sharing one `Timestamp` AND one `SignalId` but different `InstanceId` are BOTH admitted (a `Timestamp`-only key or a `SignalId`-only key each collapse this to one; both mutations were run and both fail `A69c` alone), and its header is deliberately column-shuffled to prove the by-name parse; `A69d` covers `D-3` (a) (an identity-less row still dedups by `Timestamp`) and `AC-8` (a synthetic v6 file carrying `SUCCESS`/`ADVERSE_HIT`/`PENDING`/`NO_DATA` rows migrates to v7 with every `EvalOutcome`/`TargetEverHit`/`FavBar`/`AdvBar`/`ExecResolution` value byte-for-byte unchanged) as two separate `Check()`s under one `Sub`, since both are independently load-bearing. **Every mutation named above was RUN, not reasoned** (`KeyFor` reverted to `Timestamp`-alone, reverted to `SignalId`-alone, the in-loop `existingTs.Add` removed, the `D-3` fallback replaced with a fresh GUID per call, `FormatEvalEntry` forced to write `TargetEverHit="0"` unconditionally) — each failed exactly the fixture(s) it should have and nothing else, then was reverted. Harness **339 → 344** — five `Check()`s, not four, because `A69d` carries two independently-meaningful assertions rather than the one the spec's own `AC-2` line predicted (339→343); noted here so the discrepancy is not mistaken for a miscount later. `verify-gate.ps1 -Mode local-fast` **GATE PASSED** (solution + AutoTweaker + WhatIfRunner + CeilingAudit + BacktestRunner + OrderCheck all `Build succeeded` 0/0 Release; display-parity clean — no snapshot/card surface touched; version-bump nudge: no engine-path flag, consistent with the `settings.json`-untouched design). ⚠ **Not measured: the §6.1 rendered-count effect against a real book.** The current production corpus carries zero same-second duplicate timestamps (§2.4 of the spec, **carried, not re-measured this session** — no AWS copy-back was available), so the dedup-key change is provably dormant on the live tape today; `A69a`–`A69c` are the synthetic substitute proof of what happens the day a collision occurs. **`SignalId`'s per-`InstanceId` reset WAS re-verified** this session by reading `Core/ProcessIdentity.vb` directly (`_signalId` is one `Shared Long`, minted once per process, `Interlocked.Increment`-only — confirms Trap 2 rather than carrying it). Uncommitted at authoring time — orchestrator commits per `S-4` §8 (*"Run any git write command — commit is the orchestrator's"*); this row and the two reports (`s4-batch-summary.md`, `s4-spec-back.md`) are meant to land in the same commit as the code. ⛔⛔ **ORCHESTRATOR REVIEW FOUND A BLOCKING REGRESSION IN THE FIRST BUILD AND IT SHIPPED FIXED — `R-4`. `KeyFor` chooses its namespace PER ROW and prefixes them `"ID\|"` / `"TS\|"`, and the first build's comment called the two spaces *"provably disjoint"* as if that were a feature. It is the defect: dedup needs the CACHE side and the INCOMING side to share a namespace, and in production they do not.** ⭐ **MEASURED on the box 2026-09-08 (`CommandId fd55334e…`): the eval cache holds 74,518 rows of which ZERO carry an identity (all `TS\|`), `analysis_log.csv` holds 6,696 of which ALL carry one (all `ID\|`), and 3,308 log rows are ALREADY in the cache by timestamp. Under a single-set key not one of them can match, so every engine start re-admits all 3,308 as duplicates — and `eager_backfill_on_startup` is `true` in shipped `settings.json`, so that is EVERY start.** ✅ **FIX: two sets, not one — `existingTs` (identity keys of cached rows that HAVE identity) + `existingLegacyTs` (timestamps of those that LACK it); a row is a duplicate iff either matches. `HasIdentity` is the ONE definition of the predicate, used by both `KeyFor` and the split, so they cannot drift.** ⚠ **Residual, stated: a legacy cached row at timestamp X still blocks a genuinely different new signal at X — that is exactly the PRE-`S-4` behaviour, so no regression, and it decays as identity-bearing rows replace legacy ones.** ⭐⭐ **WHY IT SURVIVED THE FIRST BUILD, and it is the reusable lesson: `A69a`–`A69d` every one build a cache in the SAME namespace as the incoming rows (all identity, or all legacy). Production is NEITHER. The five are simply empty on every pre-change row, which is the identical shape a no-episode row writes. ⛔ **Do NOT copy the v64 entry's wording** — that one could claim `analysis_log.csv` was untouched; this one cannot. **Mechanism:** four of the five were already live state on `LevelAbsorptionTracker.SideState` (`PullLB`/`PostLB`/`SizeStart`/`SizeMin`) and were discarded in `ReadSide`; only `AbsorptionEpisodeSec` needs new state — `SideState.EpisodeStartMs`, set at episode open beside `LevelPrice`, cleared in `CloseEpisode()`, with `nowMs` threaded into `ReadSide` from its single call site `Snapshot` (R2 — no clock read inside the tracker, so reads stay deterministic and both sides of one snapshot agree). Both stamps come from the same receive-time clock, so `EpisodeSec` is non-negative by construction, not by clamping. The five thread `SideState` → `ReadSide` → `AbsorptionSideRead` → `AbsorptionSnapshot` → `IndicatorEngine.ClassifyAbsorption` → `AbsorptionRead` → `r.Absorption*` → `IndicatorResults` → `AnalysisLogger`, following `PullFrac` as the template. **Three-file schema rotation (R5):** `AnalysisLogger.vb` header + values, the byte-verbatim twin `tools/BacktestRunner/BacktestRowWriter.vb`, the hardcoded third copy `tools/BacktestRunner/OverlapValidator.vb`'s `ColSpec` list (five `ColKind.Muted` entries — its `ColIndex()` resolves against that list, not the file's header), and `tools/BacktestRunner/ReplayLoop.vb` sets all five to `Nothing` on the replay path. **`Double?` + `InvOpt` throughout (R4)** — a no-episode row renders EMPTY, never `0`; a zero `EpisodeSec` and a zero `SizeStart` are both legitimate values, so `0` would be a fabricated measurement the study cannot tell from a real one. **CSV-only (R3)** — `ComposeAbsorption` in `UI/MainForm_LiveStrip.vb` is untouched and none of the five reaches any rendered surface, so the display-string parity rule is satisfied by statement. **Scoring untouched** — `absorption.scoring_enabled` stays `false` (D-5). **`A43e` fix, forced by R1:** that fixture located `InstanceId`/`SignalId` by TRAILING position (`Length - 2` / `Length - 1`), which only worked while they were the last two columns; it now resolves both by header name (the `A31f` pattern) and asserts row width explicitly — a strictly stronger guard than trailing indexing gave. It was the ONLY trailing-position CSV reader in the tree; the other 16 files in the reader audit resolve by header name or do not parse rows. **Fixtures A60a–e** (A60e written first — the only one that catches the two silent schema-twin traps), each mutation-tested: removing the `CloseEpisode` reset fails A60c and nothing else, breaking either twin header fails A60e while `A43e` still passes, writing `0` instead of empty fails A60b, dropping a field at the `ClassifyAbsorption` hop fails A60a, and moving `SignalId` fails A60d. ⚠ **`EnsureLogFile` DOES rotate on this header change, and its `.bak` name is the hardcoded `analysis_log.csv.v0.7.bak` string — already wrong for the v0.8 book and left alone deliberately** (renaming it is a behaviour change no ruling covers; the file is never deleted, and an existing `.bak` gets a timestamped name rather than being overwritten). **Next:** D-2 is gated behind ~2 weekday-weeks of these columns. |

Source: `docs/DeribitIndicatorProject.md` lines 405-405 at git tag `doc-trim-2026-09-14-pre` - 5461 B - SHA-256 `df9cfe4ef77ecba75698c738495dbee24a30906ae624ba2a2e3c24fbce2b8858`.

<!-- trim-2026-09-14-21 begin -->
| **C1-coverage F1 — trailing-edge gap mis-attribution fix** (D-1–D-6 + D-5.1–D-5.5, settings-untouched) | 2026-08-26 | **`CoverageReport.AccumulateHourStats` charges a silence gap to the hour containing the trade that ENDS it, so an hour with trades early then quiet to its own end read `Captured` — the FOLLOWING hour still flagged, but this hour's own trailing silence was invisible.** NO settings keys, NO version bump — settings stays v68. Spec: [`coverage-trailing-edge-f1-proposal.md`](coverage-trailing-edge-f1-proposal.md) §4b (BUILD-AUTHORIZED 2026-08-25; §4 is the pre-tick record, superseded on D-3 and D-6 — build from §4b only). **Mechanism:** new `HourClass.TrailingEdge` (D-5(c)), inserted between `Defect` and `ExpectedMissing` in the enum declaration (shifts four ordinals — verified safe, every reference in the tree is by name) and between `Defect` and `Captured` in `ClassifyHour`'s split-hour worst-of combine (D-5.1). `HourStoreStats` gains `LastTsMs As Long?` (D-2/D-3(c) — nullable, not a `0`/`-1` sentinel, so every hand-built fixture that never sets it is protected by the type). `ClassifySpan` (shared by the whole-hour and split paths, D-1) flags `TrailingEdge` when a span is otherwise clean but silent from its last trade to the observed end past `gap_ms`. The observed end is bounded (D-4(c)) by `MIN(the span's own end, the evidence/request boundary, the store's own last in-range trade)` — `AccumulateHourStats` now also returns `StoreEndMs As Long?`, filtered to `[fromUtc, toUtc)` so a trade sitting past the walked range in the same whole-month file can never leak in and un-exempt the true last walked hour (the superset trap, §4a.1). `BuildResult` resolves this bound ONCE and threads it through every `ClassifyHour` call as a new `Optional observedBoundMs As Long = Long.MaxValue` parameter — the default keeps every pre-F1 direct caller (fixtures that never set `LastTsMs`) running unchanged. `--strict` stays keyed on `HourClass.Defect` alone, unmoved by design (D-5.2) — **proved, not assumed:** run both ways against the AWS store copy (`AWS-copybacks/aws-copyback-2026-08-14`, ~12 days stale, the freshest local copy available), `Defect` count and `--strict` exit code identical pre-fix vs post-fix (4 defect hours, exit 1, both). The VERDICT line now also gates on `TrailingEdge` count (D-5.3) — a report with trailing-edge hours and zero `Defect` hours no longer prints `clean`. A seventh console count line (D-5.4) and a `TrailingEdgeHours`/`ObservedLongestTrailingMs` pair reported BESIDE — never folded into — `ObservedLongestGapMs`/`GapBreachHours` (D-6(c), re-ruled from the pre-tick D-6(a) after the code showed folding is a no-op on one counter and a silent double-count on the other). `BuildMarkdown` renders the new class for free (only `Captured`/`OutOfScopeWeekend` are skipped); confirmed live on all three surfaces with a synthetic trailing-edge hour (console count line, markdown table row, VERDICT line). The pre-existing placeholder note documenting the defect (`CoverageReport.vb` ~:1100) is deleted. **Fixture revisions, all named because the implementer's own change forces them:** `A49e` (calls `ClassifyHour` directly, bypassing `BuildResult` — now passes the real `StoreEndMs` as `observedBoundMs` so the fixture's original inter-trade-gap-threshold intent survives); `A49o`/`A49t` (real accumulated `LastTsMs` now populates on their ON-half spans — ~27 min and ~10 min trailing edges that would wrongly flag without the same explicit store-end bound). `A49b`/`A49c`/`A49l`/`A49r` needed **no edit** — hand-built `HourStoreStats` never set the new nullable field, so it defaults to `Nothing` and the trailing check never evaluates. **Six new fixtures (F1-a…f)**, `F1-a`/`F1-b`/`F1-d`/`F1-e` mutation-proofed per the spec's own §6 list (guard reverted or the specific alternate mechanism substituted, confirmed FAIL, restored, confirmed PASS): `F1-a` the canonical shape (flags); `F1-b` the bound's first witness (store ends there ⇒ exempt); `F1-c` the partial-final-hour edge (measures to the boundary instant, not `:59:59.999`); `F1-d` the split-hour precedence trap (D-5.1 — the ONLY fixture in the whole table that fails when the combine order is swapped; every other split-hour fixture stays green under that same mutation); `F1-e` the store-end superset trap (D-4's second witness — a `Max()` over the whole-month-file dictionary instead of the walk's own filtered last trade un-exempts the true last hour); `F1-f` the VERDICT-line gate (D-5.3). Acceptance: solution + AutoTweaker + WhatIfRunner + CeilingAudit + BacktestRunner + OrderCheck build **0/0** Release (each run separately); harness **ALL PASS** (306/306), A1–A59e unregressed, all six F1 fixtures confirmed EXECUTED (not just present); `verify-gate.ps1 -Mode local-fast` **GATE PASSED** (harness ALL PASS, display-parity clean — this is a `tools/`-only CLI change, the app's `MainForm_PlaintextSnapshot.vb`/`MainForm_Render_Cards.vb` are untouched, so no card surface is affected — version-bump nudge satisfied, no engine-path change). Real AWS-copy run over the marker-covered window reports zero genuine trailing-edge hours — a legitimate finding (the store is a continuously-streaming capture; a clean-but-silent-to-hour-end shape is rare outside restarts), independently confirmed reachable via the synthetic F1-a scenario. Local commit — trader tests + pushes. |
